- **deliberate-practice**: Activate when: user says 'I've been doing this for years but I'm not getting better'; someone suspects a skill plateau despite continued effort; designing a learning program for a high-performance outcome; an organization reports high training hours but low skill transfer. - **deliberate-practice**: 刻意练习框架——设计练习方案、诊断瓶颈、拆解技能。当用户说"怎么练""如何提升""练习没效果""怎么变厉害""训练计划"时触发，想提升任何能力时使用。属于 ymodel 体系，前置 competence-reflection 做瓶颈诊断。 ([Jinhong270/AI-Agent](https://github.com/Jinhong270/AI-Agent/tree/HEAD/Xiao-Q/skills/deliberate-practice) · ★4)
- **deliberate-practice**: Use when asked to design, audit, or fix practice so it actually builds skill — a goal just past current ability, full concentration, immediate specific feedback, and repetition with correction — or when someone has practiced for a long time without improving. ([skills-on-ai/skills-on-ai](https://github.com/skills-on-ai/skills-on-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/deliberate-practice) · ★3)
- **deliberate-practice**: 用 Akraken 在 Deadlock 讲座中讲的刻意练习方法，帮用户制定变强计划。Use when 用户说“怎么刻意练习”“我想比别人更快变强”“帮我定训练计划”或“怎么复盘”。不用于总结视频、制作 PPT、制作口播、只下载视频，或把研究转述改写成已证实的普遍规律。 ([yhyu13/AgentSkillsVault](https://github.com/yhyu13/AgentSkillsVault/tree/HEAD/SKILL/knowledge-vault/tier0/deliberate-practice) · ★3)
- **deliberate-practice**: Deliberate Practice (Ericsson): targeted, edge-of-ability exercises with immediate feedback ([AnjanJ/bodhikit](https://github.com/AnjanJ/bodhikit/tree/HEAD/skills/deliberate-practice) · ★2)
- **deliberate-practice**: Deliberate Practice — Anders Ericsson's theory of deliberate practice. - **deliberate-practice**: Apply deliberate practice principles for rapid skill acquisition and expert-level performance. - **deliberate-practice**: 当用户想改进任何技能的练习方式时调用：练了很久没进步、卡在瓶颈、不知道怎么练、没人教只能自学、坚持不下去、怀疑自己或孩子“没天赋”、担心“年纪大了来不及”。 覆盖《刻意练习》（艾利克森 & 普尔）的全部方法论：有目的的练习四特点（目标/专注/反馈/走出舒适区）诊断与改造、挑战校准（舒适区边缘与难度调节）、瓶颈突破（换方式先于加难度）、无导师自训（三步路线图与三个F、复写-对比-纠正）、心理表征（功能诊断与创建）、动机系统（弱化停下理由＋增强前行理由）、目标分解与进步监测、导师的选择与更换、工作中训练化与组织训练系统、教学与课程改造、天赋偏见纠偏（自我实现预言与标签）、天才传说祛魅（两问框架）、1万小时法则纠错、成年与大龄学习判断、杰出人物路线图与“练习人”心法。 不适用于：临床心理与精神疾病诊断、正规教育升学与职业资格决策、把书中转述的心理学机制当作临床或医学结论使用。 ([AholicK/zh-book-skills](https://github.com/AholicK/zh-book-skills/tree/HEAD/deliberate-practice))
- **deliberate-practice**: Designs and runs practice sessions with one specific target at the edge of current ability: the assistant builds the drill — a debugging kata, a negotiation roleplay, a writing constraint exercise, a cross-examination rehearsal — plays the environment or opponent, gives immediate feedback that names the gap, and raises difficulty only on demonstrated competence, logging the target → attempt → feedback → next-target chain. - **deliberate-practice-plan**: Design deliberate practice that actually builds a skill — targeted, effortful, feedback-driven — instead of mindless repetition that just entrenches your current level. - **deling-knowledge-skill**: 用于访问德灵知识库平台并进行深度调研的专用 skill。当用户明确提到「德灵」「deling」「知识库检索」「深度研究」等关键词，或者需要进行系统化内容调研时，应优先调用本 skill。本 skill 支持：基于德灵知识库的系统化深度调研、知识库内容语义搜索、辅助网络搜索。 ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/02_%E7%9F%A5%E8%AF%86%E8%B5%84%E4%BA%A7/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E5%BD%92%E6%A1%A3/deling-knowledge-skill))

- **Delinquency and Collections**: Canonical delinquency-to-resolution playbook. Classifies every case by aging stage, proposes the next-step action per the overlay's playbook, generates draft resident communications with legal-review banners where jurisdictions treat them as notices, opens approval_requests for every gated transition (legal notice, eviction filing, non-standard payment plan, write-off above threshold), and records fair-housing guardrails at each decision point. Runs weekly at the property and on-demand when a single case transitions aging buckets. ([mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin](https://github.com/mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/src/skills/residential_multifamily/workflows/delinquency_collections) · ★52)
- **delippy-api-structure**: Hướng dẫn cấu trúc API (Request -&gt; Controller -&gt; Service -&gt; Resource), cấu hình & sử dụng Rate Limiting, cơ chế xử lý ngoại lệ cho Thin Controller, và các mẫu thiết kế nâng cao (Stateless Calculators, Smart Enums, Composite Pattern, Webhook Safety). ([ngocthanhnt00/livestream-demo](https://github.com/ngocthanhnt00/livestream-demo/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/base-structure-source-backend))
